In Excel, this can be implemented using Solver to minimize an objective function.\n\n### HP Filter Formula\nThe objective function to minimize is:\n```\nΣ[(y_t - trend_t)²] + λ * Σ[(trend_t+1 - trend_t) - (trend_t - trend_t-1)]²\n```\n\nWhere:\n- y_t is the original series\n- trend_t is the smoothed trend\n- λ (lambda) is the smoothing parameter (typically 100 for annual data, 1600 for quarterly)\n- The second term is the second-order difference (measures curvature)\n\n## Excel Implementation Steps\n\n### 1. Setup Data Structure\n- Column with original data (e.g., LnZ)\n- Column for trend values (LnZ_HP) - these are the changing cells\n- Column for first differences: `=trend_t - trend_{t-1}`\n- Column for second differences: `=first_diff_t - first_diff_{t-1}`\n- Column for deviations: `=(original_t - trend_t)^2`\n- Column for curvature: `=second_diff_t^2`\n\n### 2. Calculate Objective Function\n```excel\n=SUM(deviations) + lambda * SUM(curvature)\n```\n\n### 3. Solver Configuration\n- **Set Objective**: Cell containing objective function\n- **To**: Min\n- **By Changing Variable Cells**: Range of trend values\n- **Solving Method**: GRG Nonlinear\n\n### 4. Initial Values\nStart with trend values equal to original values to help Solver converge.\n\n## Common Lambda Values\n- Annual data: λ = 100\n- Quarterly data: λ = 1600\n- Monthly data: λ = 14400\n\n## Tips\n- Ensure there are no circular references\n- Use named ranges for clarity\n- Start with trend = original data\n- Check that second-order differences are calculated correctly (skip first 2 rows)\n- Verify objective function includes all terms\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1301,"cache_read_input_tokens":18094,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1301,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4d5a8424-002e-41cc-a013-084ca824ad0d","uuid":"e17375e4-0765-4b58-b3ec-ad197897c22e"}

α*ln(K) - (1-α)*ln(L)\n```\n\nOr simplified (assuming labor is normalized or absorbed into A):\n```\nln(Z) = ln(Y) - α*ln(K)\n```\nWhere Z represents TFP adjusted for labor.\n\n## Potential GDP Calculation\n\n### Given Known TFP Trend\n```\nY* = exp(ln(Z_trend)) * K^α\n```\n\nOr in log form:\n```\nln(Y*) = ln(Z_trend) + α*ln(K)\n```\n\nWhere:\n- Y* = Potential GDP\n- ln(Z_trend) = Smoothed TFP trend (from HP filter)\n- α = Capital's share\n\n## Capital Stock Dynamics\n\n### Perpetual Inventory Method\n```\nK_t = K_{t-1} * (1 - δ) + I_t\n```\n\nWhere:\n- K_t = Capital stock at time t\n- δ = Depreciation rate\n- I_t = Investment at time t\n\n### Depreciation Rate Calculation\n```\nδ = CFC / K\n```\n\nWhere:\n- CFC = Consumption of Fixed Capital\n- K = Capital stock\n\n## Capital-Output Ratio\n\n### As Steady-State Anchor\n```\nK/Y ratio = average of recent years\n```\n\nUsed to project future capital stock:\n```\nK_t = (K/Y ratio) * Y_t\n```\n\n## Investment Shock Analysis\n\n### Baseline Scenario\nProject capital and output without additional investment\n\n### Shock Scenario\nAdd investment shock to capital accumulation:\n```\nK_with_t = K_base_{t-1} * (1 - δ) + I_base_t + I_shock_t\n```\n\nThen calculate new potential output:\n```\nY*_with_t = exp(ln(Z_trend_t)) * K_with_t^α\n```\n\n### Impact Calculation\n```\nGDP Impact = Y*_with - Y*_base\nImpact % = (Y*_with / Y*_base - 1) * 100\n```\n\n## Common Parameters\n- Capital share (α): 0.35-0.40 for most economies\n- Depreciation rate (δ): 0.05-0.08 annually\n- Investment shock: Distributed over project period\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1301,"cache_read_input_tokens":18094,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1301,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4d5a8424-002e-41cc-a013-084ca824ad0d","uuid":"60a50176-f3f3-4502-ade4-92fb52ec8488"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01Pd4NHHPk6eiNsQUWp3ZbVd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_019KaJajkCP83B9MVs6o8xnJ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/excel-formula-patterns.md","content":"# Excel Formula Patterns for Economic Data Analysis\n\n## Data Linking Between Sheets\n\n### Basic Reference\n```excel\n='SheetName'!A1\n```\n\n### Range Reference\n```excel\n='SheetName'!A1:A10\n```\n\n### Dynamic Reference with OFFSET\n```excel\n=OFFSET(SheetName!$A$1, row_offset, col_offset, height, width)\n```\n\n## Time Series Calculations\n\n### Growth Rate\n```excel\n=(Y_t / Y_{t-1}) - 1\n```\n\nOr as percentage:\n```excel\n=(Y_t / Y_{t-1} - 1) * 100\n```\n\n### Extending Projections\nIf growth rate is constant after certain year:\n```excel\n=PreviousYear * (1 + growth_rate)\n```\n\n### Cumulative Growth\n```excel\n=BaseYear * (1 + growth_rate)^years\n```\n\n## Statistical Functions\n\n### Average of Recent N Years\n```excel\n=AVERAGE(range_of_last_N_cells)\n```\n\n### Moving Average\n```excel\n=AVERAGE(OFFSET(current_cell, -N+1, 0, N, 1))\n```\n\n## Logarithmic Calculations\n\n### Natural Log\n```excel\n=LN(value)\n```\n\n### Exponential (inverse of LN)\n```excel\n=EXP(ln_value)\n```\n\n## Trend Extrapolation\n\n### TREND Function\nExtends a linear trend based on known values:\n```excel\n=TREND(known_y_values, known_x_values, new_x_values)\n```\n\nExample for time series:\n```excel\n=TREND($B$2:$B$20, $A$2:$A$20, A21)\n```\n\n### For Log-Linear Trends\n1. Calculate known ln(Y) values\n2. Use TREND to extend ln(Y)\n3. Apply EXP to get Y values\n\n```excel\n=EXP(TREND(LN(known_y), known_x, new_x))\n```\n\n## Conditional Formulas\n\n### IF Statement\n```excel\n=IF(condition, value_if_true, value_if_false)\n```\n\n### Nested IF for Multiple Conditions\n```excel\n=IF(year<=2023, historical_value, IF(year<=2033, projection1, projection2))\n```\n\n## Array Formulas (Legacy)\n\n### SUM of Products\n```excel\n=SUM(range1 * range2)\n```\nEnter with Ctrl+Shift+Enter in older Excel versions.\n\n## Named Ranges\n\n### Benefits\n- Easier to read formulas\n- Easier to maintain\n- Reduce errors\n\n### Usage\nInstead of:\n```excel\n=SUM(Sheet1!$B$2:$B$100)\n```\n\nUse:\n```excel\n=SUM(GDP_Data)\n```\n\n## Common Patterns for This Task\n\n### Capital Stock Projection\n```excel\n=K_previous * (1 - depreciation_rate) + Investment\n```\n\n### Potential GDP from Production Function\n```excel\n=EXP(LnZ_trend) * K^alpha\n```\n\nOr in log space:\n```excel\n=EXP(LnZ_trend + alpha * LN(K))\n```\n\n### Second-Order Difference\n```excel\n=(value_t - value_{t-1}) - (value_{t-1} - value_{t-2})\n```\n\nOr:\n```excel\n=value_t - 2*value_{t-1} + value_{t-2}\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1301,"cache_read_input_tokens":18094,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1301,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4d5a8424-002e-41cc-a013-084ca824ad0d","uuid":"1fed600e-cf6e-415d-8e2a-3cecbbf6dbea"}

α*ln(K) - (1-α)*ln(L)\n```\n\nOr simplified (assuming labor is normalized or absorbed into A):\n```\nln(Z) = ln(Y) - α*ln(K)\n```\nWhere Z represents TFP adjusted for labor.\n\n## Potential GDP Calculation\n\n### Given Known TFP Trend\n```\nY* = exp(ln(Z_trend)) * K^α\n```\n\nOr in log form:\n```\nln(Y*) = ln(Z_trend) + α*ln(K)\n```\n\nWhere:\n- Y* = Potential GDP\n- ln(Z_trend) = Smoothed TFP trend (from HP filter)\n- α = Capital's share\n\n## Capital Stock Dynamics\n\n### Perpetual Inventory Method\n```\nK_t = K_{t-1} * (1 - δ) + I_t\n```\n\nWhere:\n- K_t = Capital stock at time t\n- δ = Depreciation rate\n- I_t = Investment at time t\n\n### Depreciation Rate Calculation\n```\nδ = CFC / K\n```\n\nWhere:\n- CFC = Consumption of Fixed Capital\n- K = Capital stock\n\n## Capital-Output Ratio\n\n### As Steady-State Anchor\n```\nK/Y ratio = average of recent years\n```\n\nUsed to project future capital stock:\n```\nK_t = (K/Y ratio) * Y_t\n```\n\n## Investment Shock Analysis\n\n### Baseline Scenario\nProject capital and output without additional investment\n\n### Shock Scenario\nAdd investment shock to capital accumulation:\n```\nK_with_t = K_base_{t-1} * (1 - δ) + I_base_t + I_shock_t\n```\n\nThen calculate new potential output:\n```\nY*_with_t = exp(ln(Z_trend_t)) * K_with_t^α\n```\n\n### Impact Calculation\n```\nGDP Impact = Y*_with - Y*_base\nImpact % = (Y*_with / Y*_base - 1) * 100\n```\n\n## Common Parameters\n- Capital share (α): 0.35-0.40 for most economies\n- Depreciation rate (δ): 0.05-0.08 annually\n- Investment shock: Distributed over project period\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Calculate known ln(Y) values\n2. Use TREND to extend ln(Y)\n3. Apply EXP to get Y values\n\n```excel\n=EXP(TREND(LN(known_y), known_x, new_x))\n```\n\n## Conditional Formulas\n\n### IF Statement\n```excel\n=IF(condition, value_if_true, value_if_false)\n```\n\n### Nested IF for Multiple Conditions\n```excel\n=IF(year<=2023, historical_value, IF(year<=2033, projection1, projection2))\n```\n\n## Array Formulas (Legacy)\n\n### SUM of Products\n```excel\n=SUM(range1 * range2)\n```\nEnter with Ctrl+Shift+Enter in older Excel versions.\n\n## Named Ranges\n\n### Benefits\n- Easier to read formulas\n- Easier to maintain\n- Reduce errors\n\n### Usage\nInstead of:\n```excel\n=SUM(Sheet1!$B$2:$B$100)\n```\n\nUse:\n```excel\n=SUM(GDP_Data)\n```\n\n## Common Patterns for This Task\n\n### Capital Stock Projection\n```excel\n=K_previous * (1 - depreciation_rate) + Investment\n```\n\n### Potential GDP from Production Function\n```excel\n=EXP(LnZ_trend) * K^alpha\n```\n\nOr in log space:\n```excel\n=EXP(LnZ_trend + alpha * LN(K))\n```\n\n### Second-Order Difference\n```excel\n=(value_t - value_{t-1}) - (value_{t-1} - value_{t-2})\n```\n\nOr:\n```excel\n=value_t - 2*value_{t-1} + value_{t-2}\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Extract capital stock (cn or rnna)\n2. Extract GDP (rgdpo or rgdpe)\n3. Extract labor share (labsh) to calculate capital share\n4. Extract years of data available\n\n### Data Format\n- Usually provided as CSV or Excel\n- Rows: Countries\n- Columns: Variables\n- Multiple years in separate columns or long format\n\n### Country Codes\n- ISO 3-letter codes (e.g., GEO for Georgia)\n- Country names may vary\n\n## File Structure\n\n### Typical PWT File\n```\ncountry | countrycode | year | rgdpe | rgdpo | pop | emp | avh | ...\n```\n\n### Metadata Sheet\nUsually includes:\n- Variable definitions\n- Units of measurement\n- Data sources\n- Methodology notes\n- Version information\n\n## Best Practices\n\n### Data Selection\n- Use output-side GDP (rgdpo) for production function analysis\n- Use capital stock at current PPPs (cn) for consistency\n- Check data availability by year for your country\n\n### Quality Checks\n- Verify year range covers your analysis period\n- Check for missing values\n- Ensure units are consistent (typically millions)\n- Confirm PPP base year (usually 2017 USD)\n\n### Conversion Needs\nIf PWT uses millions and you need billions:\n```excel\n=PWT_value / 1000\n```\n\n## Latest Version\nAs of 2025, PWT 10.01 or later should be available with data through 2019-2023.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Data Collection ✓\n- **Penn World Table (PWT) 11.0**: Downloaded from https://www.rug.nl/ggdc/productivity/pwt/\n  - Capital stock (cn): 2000-2023\n  - Real GDP output-side (rgdpo): 2000-2023\n  - Labor share (labsh): 2000-2023\n\n- **IMF World Economic Outlook (WEO)**: Retrieved via IMF API\n  - Real GDP growth rates: 2000-2030\n  - Calculated real GDP levels from base year 2000\n  - Extended projections to 2043 using 2027 growth rate (5%)\n\n- **ECB Consumption of Fixed Capital**: Retrieved via ECB API\n  - Annual CFC data for Georgia: 2000-2023\n  - Used to calculate depreciation rates\n\n### 2. Excel Workbook Structure ✓\n\nThe file `test-supply.xlsx` contains 6 sheets:\n\n#### Instructions Sheet\n- Comprehensive user guide\n- Data sources documentation\n- Solver setup instructions\n- Column explanations\n\n#### PWT Sheet\n- Columns: Year, Capital Stock, Labor Share, Real GDP\n- Years: 2000-2023\n- Raw data from Penn World Table\n\n#### WEO_Data Sheet\n- Columns: Year, Growth Rate (%), Real GDP Level\n- Years: 2000-2043\n- Formulas extend GDP projections using growth rates\n\n#### CFC data Sheet\n- Columns: Year, Note, CFC (Millions GEL), Capital Stock, Depreciation Rate\n- Depreciation rate formula: CFC/K\n- Links capital stock from PWT sheet\n\n#### Production Sheet\n**Section 1: Parameters (Rows 1-3)**\n- B2: Capital's share (alpha) = 1 - AVERAGE(labor share 2015-2023)\n- B3: Depreciation rate = AVERAGE(depreciation 2016-2023)\n\n**Section 2: Historical TFP (Rows 6-28)**\n- Columns A-G: Year, K, Y, LnK, LnY, LnZ, LnZ_HP\n- LnZ = LnY - alpha*LnK (TFP residual)\n- LnZ_HP: Smoothed trend (Solver changing cells)\n\n**Section 3: HP Filter Solver Area (Columns K-P, Rows 6-28)**\n- K: Year\n- L: LnZ_HP (links to column G)\n- M: First difference\n- N: Second difference\n- O: LnZ - Trend (deviation)\n- P: Squared deviation\n- P5: Objective function = SUM(squared deviations) + 100*SUM(squared 2nd differences)\n\n**Section 4: Projections (Rows 31-71, Years 2002-2041)**\n- A: Year\n- B: Y (GDP from WEO)\n- C: K (Capital stock, extended using K/Y ratio)\n- D: K/Y ratio\n- E: lnK\n- F: lnY\n- G: lnZ_trend (TREND function extrapolates from HP filtered series)\n- H: Ystar_base = EXP(lnZ_trend + alpha*lnK)\n- I: Investment shock (from Investment sheet)\n- J: deltaK (depreciation)\n- K: K_with (capital with investment shock)\n- L: lnK_with\n- M: Ystar_with = EXP(lnZ_trend + alpha*lnK_with)\n- N: Impact (absolute)\n- O: Impact (%)\n\n#### Investment Sheet\n- Total investment: $6,500 million USD\n- Period: 2026-2033 (8 years)\n- Annual investment: $812.5 million/year\n- Schedule table for years 2002-2041\n\n### 3. Key Features ✓\n\n**All Formulas Preserved**\n- No hardcoded numbers in calculation cells\n- 829 formulas in Production sheet\n- 48 formulas in CFC data sheet\n- 16 formulas in WEO_Data sheet\n- All calculations are dynamic and transparent\n\n**Cobb-Douglas Production Function**\n- Y* = A * K^α\n- Log form: ln(Y*) = ln(A) + α*ln(K)\n- α (capital's share) calculated from data\n- TFP (A) estimated using HP filter\n\n**HP Filter Implementation**\n- Lambda = 100 (standard for annual data)\n- Objective minimizes: deviations² + λ*(2nd differences)²\n- Solver required: Minimize P5 by changing G7:G28\n\n**Capital Stock Projection**\n- Historical: PWT data (2000-2023)\n- Future: K = (K/Y ratio) * Y\n- K/Y ratio = average of 2015-2023\n\n**TFP Trend Extrapolation**\n- Uses Excel TREND() function\n- Based on HP filtered series (2002-2024)\n- Linear extrapolation to 2041\n\n### 4. Critical Next Step: Run Solver ⚠️\n\nThe HP filter MUST be run using Excel Solver before the results are valid.\n\n**Solver Settings:**\n1. Open Production sheet\n2. Data tab → Solver\n3. Set Objective: Cell P5\n4. To: Min\n5. By Changing Variable Cells: G7:G28\n6. Solving Method: GRG Nonlinear\n7. Click Solve\n\nThis will smooth the TFP series and enable accurate projections.\n\n### 5.
